only)\n\n**Actor References:**\n- `FActorReference` - Stable reference\n  - Resolution order: GUID -> Path -> Name -> Label\n  - `AActor::GetActorGuid()` - Get GUID\n  - `AActor::GetPathName()` - Get full path\n  - `AActor::GetName()` - Get internal name\n\n**Asset Registry (WITH_EDITOR):**\n- `FAssetRegistryModule::Get()` - Get asset registry\n- `IAssetRegistry::GetAssetsByClass()` - Find assets by type\n- `FAssetData` - Asset metadata\n\n#### Key UE Functions Called\n\n**World/Context:**\n- `GetWorld()` - Get owning world from actor/component\n- `GWorld` - Global world pointer (packaged game only)\n- `FWorldDelegates::OnPreWorldInitialization()` - World lifecycle\n- `FEditorDelegates::OnPostEngineInit()` - Editor startup\n\n**Actor Queries:**\n- `TActorIterator<T>()` - Iterate loaded actors\n- `FWorldPartitionHelpers::ForEachActorDescInstance()` - Iterate all metadata (WITH_EDITOR)\n- `UWorld::SpawnActor<T>(FActorSpawnParameters)` - Spawn with location/rotation\n- `AActor::Destroy()` - Delete actor\n- `FindObject<AActor>()` - Find by path\n\n**Component Access:**\n- `AActor::FindComponentByClass<T>()` - Find component by type\n- `AActor::GetComponentByClass<T>()` - Get first component of type\n- `AActor::GetComponents()` - All components\n- `USceneComponent::AttachToComponent()` - Attach to parent\n- `USceneComponent::DetachFromComponent()` - Detach\n\n**Transforms:**\n- `AActor::SetActorLocation()` with sweep/collision options\n- `AActor::SetActorRotation()`\n- `AActor::SetActorScale3D()`\n- `USceneComponent::SetRelativeLocation/Rotation/Scale()`\n- `FTransform` structure operations\n\n**Landscape Bounds:**\n- `ALandscapeProxy::GetComponentsBoundingBox()` - Accurate bounds\n- elevation\n\n**Streaming State (WITH_EDITOR):**\n- `FWorldPartitionHandle::Get()` - Load actor on demand\n- `FWorldPartitionHandle::IsLoaded()` - Check if loaded\n- `FWorldPartitionActorDesc::GetEditorBounds()` - Metadata bounds\n- `ForEachActorDescInstance()` with bounds filtering\n\n**Data Layers:**\n- `UDataLayerManager::GetDataLayerInstances()`\n- `UDataLayerManager::GetDataLayerInstanceNames()`\n- Actor's `GetDataLayerInstanceNames()` method\n\n#### Compile Directives\n\n```cpp\n#if WITH_EDITOR\n- queries return `EActorStreamingState::NotApplicable` if no WP, iterator-based queries return empty results.\n\n---\n\n### AGENTBRIDGESCRIPTING MODULE\n\n**Purpose:** Command layer with JSON compatibility\n2. **Blueprint Node Classes** - K2Node classes may have API changes, check AllocateDefaultPins() and pin creation\n3. **Landscape APIs** - Height data access and streaming proxy behavior may change\n4. **World Partition** - Actor descriptor API and data layer system may evolve\n5. **gRPC/Protobuf Version** - TempoScriptingServer may upgrade protobuf version, check header compatibility\n6. **Editor-Only Code** - WITH_EDITOR blocks must be validated for new UE versions\n7. **Property Flags** - CPF_* flags and property attribute checks may have changes\n8. **Subsystem Lifecycle** - WorldSubsystem registration timing may differ\n\nAll compilation guards are properly wrapped in `#if WITH_EDITOR` where applicable, reducing migration risk for runtime
